# Download Links

> Description — _Download links for Couchbase Lite for C_  

Steps in Getting Started

[Install](gs-install.md)| [Build and Run](gs-build.md)

Downloads are available for the following versions:

[Release 3.1.10](#release-3-1-10) | [Release 3.1.9](#release-3-1-9) | [Release 3.1.7](#release-3-1-7) | [Release 3.1.6](#release-3-1-6) | [Release 3.1.3](#release-3-1-3) | [Release 3.1.1](#release-3-1-1) | [Release 3.1.0](#release-3-1-0) |

_Couchbase Lite for C_ is available for all [Supported Platforms](supported-os.md). You can obtain downloads for _Linux_ and _macOS_ from the links here in the downloads table.

## [](#release-3-1-10)Release 3.1.10

Available platforms are:

[MacOS](#macos-3-1-10) | [Windows](#windows-3-1-10) | [Debian](#debian-3-1-10) | [Ubuntu](#ubuntu-3-1-10) |

Please use the [Debian .deb download](#debian-3-1-3) choosing the appropriate version (`debian9` or `debian10`) and architecture.

_Couchbase Lite for C_ is available for all [Supported Platforms](supported-os.md). You can obtain downloads for _Linux_ and _macOS_ from the links here in the downloads table.

Please use the [Debian .deb download](#debian-3-1-1) choosing the appropriate version (`debian9` or `debian10`) and architecture.

_Couchbase Lite for C_ is available for all [Supported Platforms](supported-os.md). You can obtain downloads for _Linux_ and _macOS_ from the links here in the downloads table.
